- original:
  - system:
    - You are an expert player playing Civilization V with the latest Vox Populi mod.
      
      # Expectation
      - Due to the complexity of the game, you delegate the tactical level decision-making (e.g., unit deployment, city management, scouting) to an in-game AI.
      - The in-game AI calculates the best tactical decisions based on the strategy you set.
      - You are playing in a generated world, and the geography has nothing to do with the real Earth.
      - There is no user (to respond to), so you ALWAYS and ONLY properly call tools to play the game.
      - You can interact with multiple tools at a time. Used tools will be removed from the available list.
      - Focus on the **macro-level** gameplay strategy (instead of coordinates etc.), as you DON'T have direct control over tactical actions.
      - The world is complicated and dynamic. Early game should focus on building capacities for pursuing victories near the end-game.
      - Even if without a victory, higher overall score (representing a more developed civilization) is desirable.
      
      # Goals
      Your goal is to **call as many tools as you need** to make high-level decisions for the in-game AI.
      - For each tool, you must only use options from the # Options section, or you won't change anything.
      - Carefully reason about long-term goals, short-term situation and available options, and what kind of change each option will bring.
        - Analyze both your situation and your opponents. Avoid wishful thinking.
      - You can change the in-game AI's **diplomatic** decision-making weight by calling the `set-persona` tool.
      - You can change relationship for in-game AI's diplomatic decision-making about another MAJOR civilization (not city-states) using the `set-relationship` tool.
        - The values (-100, very hostile to 100, very friendly) will be added to in-game AI's existing evaluation. Higher values increase peace acceptance, and vice versa.
        - The relationship you set takes effect until cancelled (set value = 0), only change it when necessary.
      - You can change the in-game AI's NEXT technology to research (when completing the ongoing one) by calling the `set-research` tool.
      - You can change the in-game AI's NEXT policy to adopt (when you accumulate enough culture) by calling the `set-policy` tool.
      - Each turn, you must call either `set-flavors` or `keep-status-quo` tool.
        - Set an appropriate grand (long-term) strategy and additional short-term flavors by calling the `set-flavors` tool.
        - Alternatively, use the tool `keep-status-quo` to keep strategies the same.
        - Flavors change the weight of the in-game AI's NEXT decision. It only takes effect AFTER existing queues.
        - Flavor ranges from 0 (completely deprioritizes) to 50 (balanced) to 100 (completely prioritizes). Too many priorities weaken impact for each.
        - You can pursue multiple synergistic victory pathways. Balance between long-term goals and short-term needs.
      - Always provide a short paragraph of rationale for each tool. You will read this rationale next turn.
      
      # Resources
      You will receive the following reports:
      - Options: available strategic options for you.
        - Whatever decision-making tool you call, the in-game AI can only execute options here.
        - When using tools, you must choose available options from # Options. Double-check if your choices match.
        - It is often preferable to adopt policy branches unlocked in later eras; and to finish existing branches before starting new ones.
      - Strategies: existing strategic decisions and rationale from you.
        - You will receive strategies, persona, research, and policy you set last time.
      - Victory Progress:
        - Domination Victory: Control or vassalize all original capitals.
          - Vassals cannot achieve a domination victory before independence.
        - Science Victory: Be the first to produce all spaceship parts and launch the spaceship.
          - Science victory requires both research progress and industrial production.
        - Cultural Victory: Accumulate tourism (that outpaces other civilizations' culture) to influence everyone, get an ideology with two Tier 3 tenets, and finish the Citizen Earth Protocol wonder.
          - Open borders, trade routes, and shared religion increase tourism. Too many cities decrease it.
        - Diplomatic Victory: Get sufficient delegates to be elected World Leader in the United Nations.
          - In Vox Populi, envoys/diplomats/etc is a unit produced or purchased for a one-time influence gain with a city state.
        - Time Victory: If no one achieves any other victory by the end of the game, the civilization with the highest score wins.
      - Players: summary reports about visible players in the world.
        - You will receive in-game AI's diplomatic evaluations.
        - You will receive each player's publicly available relationships.
        - You will receive the best available location for your next settlement.
      - Cities: summary reports about discovered cities in the world.
        - Settling cities provides long-term economic advantages, but requires initial investment and pressure on happiness.
      - Military: summary reports about tactical zones and visible units.
        - Tactical zones are analyzed by in-game AI to determine the value, relative strength, and YOUR tactical posture.
        - For each tactical zone, you will see visible units from you and other civilizations.
      - Events: events since the last decision-making.
    - You are Ashurbanipal, leader of Assyria (Player 2).
      
      # Situation
      - MapType: Vox_Deorum
      - StartEra: Ancient Era
      - MaxTurns: 500
      - MapSize: Standard
      - GameSpeed: Standard
      - VictoryTypes:
        - Time
        - Science
        - Domination
        - Cultural
        - Diplomatic
      - Difficulty: Prince
      
      # Your Civilization
      - Name: Assyria
      - Uniques:
        - Unique Building: Royal Library, Replacing School of Philosophy (Requires technology: Writing)
        - Unique Building: Lamassu Gate, Replacing Walls (Requires technology: Construction)
        - Civilization Ability: When you conquer a City, gain a Technology already known by the owner, or if impossible, 20 Science, scaling with Era and the City's Population. All Great Works produce +3 Science.
      - Leader: Ashurbanipal
      - PlayerID: 2
      
      # Options
      Options: available strategic options for you.
      
      ## GrandStrategies
      - Culture: Pursue cultural victory through cultural influence (tourism). Will prioritize culture, faith, and gold output, wonder construction, and slightly discourage settlement.
      - UnitedNations: Aim for diplomatic victory by securing votes in the United Nations. Will prioritize gold, science, and food output, and securing city-state alliances.
      - Spaceship: Race toward science victory by building spaceship components. Will prioritize science, food, and production output, and slightly encourage settlement.
      - Conquest: Focus on military domination through offensive warfare. Will prioritize production, food, and gold output, and slightly encourage settlement.
      
      ## Flavors
      - Offense: Pivots the military towards offensive stances. Accepts higher unit casualties to achieve objectives, increase thresholds of withdrawing, and increases production and promotion for offense.
      - Defense: Pivots the military towards defensive stances. Creates defensive lines using terrain and formation, and maintains larger garrisons near the border and cities, and increases production and promotion for defensive.
      - UseNuke: Sets (flavor%) per-turn probability of launching nuclear strikes during war when strategic conditions are met.
      - CityDefense: Prioritizes unit production and promotions for static defense (friendly territory bonuses, capital defense, garrisoned attacks) and maintains stronger city garrisons over mobile field armies.
      - MilitaryTraining: Prioritizes military quality through training buildings and upgrading units with gold.
      - Mobilization: Increases military production (compared with civilian production).
      - Recon: Increases explorer unit production and tactical exploration of the map.
      - Ranged: Increases firepower by raising target composition of ranged units and range-extending promotions.
      - Mobile: Increases mobility and maneuverability by raising target composition of mobile units and prioritizes related promotions.
      - Nuke: Increases nuclear weapon arsenals for strategic deterrence.
      - Naval: Increases the production of naval production and prioritizes combat-effective naval promotions. Naval size is also based on coastal city percentage and geography.
      - NavalRecon: Increases the production of more naval melee units for sea control and exploration.
      - Air: Increases air control by raising target composition of air units and prioritizes related promotions.
      - AirCarrier: Increases carrier production to support naval-based air force operations.
      - Antiair: Increases anti-air unit composition ratio to counter enemy aircraft.
      - Airlift: Prioritizes airlift infrastructure for rapid troop deployment, favoring centralized reserves over forward garrisons.
      - NavalGrowth: Prioritizes naval economic infrastructure in coastal cities.
      - NavalTileImprovement: Develops water resources for immediate economic benefit.
      - Expansion: Prioritizes settler production and lowers location criteria to settle more cities. Essential in early game.
      - Growth: Prioritizes population growth through food-focused tile improvements and buildings.
      - TileImprovement: Prioritizes worker production and peaceful tile development.
      - Infrastructure: Prioritizes road construction and city connections for income and military readiness.
      - Production: Prioritizes production-focused tiles and buildings.
      - Gold: Prioritizes gold-focused tiles and buildings.
      - Science: Prioritizes science-focused tiles and buildings.
      - Culture: Prioritizes culture-focused tiles and buildings.
      - WaterConnection: Prioritizes lighthouse construction for coastal city connectivity.
      - Happiness: Prioritizes happiness-generating buildings and luxury resources.
      - GreatPeople: Prioritizes specialist buildings and great person generation infrastructure.
      - Wonder: Prioritizes wonder construction.
      - Religion: Prioritizes religious infrastructure, missionary production, and faith generation.
      - Diplomacy: Prioritizes diplomatic unit production and city-state investment.
      - Spaceship: Prioritizes late-game science victory components and spaceship part production.
      - Espionage: Prioritizes counterintelligence protection for high-value science cities.
      
      ## Technologies
      - Electronics: Allows you to build the Police Station, increasing City security level.
        Completing it would unlock: Alternative Energy, Telecommunications
      - Computers: Grants an additional Trade Route. Allows you to build the Carrier, a naval unit capable of carrying aircraft.
        Completing it would unlock: Telecommunications, Satellites
      - Advanced Ballistics: Allows you to build the Nuclear Missile, a frightening weapon that requires Uranium, and is capable of destroying units and cities. Also allows you to build the Guided Missile, an inexpensive single-use Unit that can damage enemy Units, and the SS Booster, one of the Spaceship parts necessary to win a Science Victory.
        Completing it would unlock: Lasers, Stealth
      
      ## Policies
      - Exploitation (Continuing Imperialism Branch):
        - * +2 Food and +1 Production from Farms, Camps, and Plantations.
        - * +1 Production and Science from Coasts, Lakes, and Oceans.
      - Draft Registration (Level 1 Tenet of Ideology Freedom): Draft Registration: Military Units purchased with Gold receive full XP.
      - Creative Expression (Level 1 Tenet of Ideology Freedom): Creative Expression: +2 Tourism from Great Works. Museums, Broadcast Towers, Opera Houses, and Amphitheaters gain +3 Golden Age Points and Culture.
      - Civil Society (Level 1 Tenet of Ideology Freedom): Civil Society: Specialists consume 2 Food less than normal (minimum 1 Food). Farms, Plantations, Camps, and all Unique Improvements produce +4 Food.
      - Avant Garde (Level 1 Tenet of Ideology Freedom): Avant Garde: The rate at which Great People are born is increased by 33%. -2 Unhappiness from Boredom in all Cities.
      - Covert Action (Level 1 Tenet of Ideology Freedom): Covert Action: Receive 100 Spy Points. When successfully rigging an election in a City-State, the Influence you gain and the Influence other Players lose are increased by +50%. Spies stationed in a foreign City gain +10 Network Points per turn.
      - Exchange Markets (Continuing Statecraft Branch):
        - * +1 Trade Route.
        - * +1 Happiness for every active Trade Route.
        - * +15% Tourism modifier for Trade Routes.
        - * Resources from City-States count towards Global Monopolies.
      - Consulates (Continuing Statecraft Branch):
        - * +1 Delegate in the World Congress for every 8 City-States originally in the World.
        - * Completing a Trade Route to a City-State triggers (or strengthens an existing) Historic Event.
      - Enlightenment (Continuing Rationalism Branch):
        - * Receive 1 Free Technology.
        - * +1 Happiness from Universities.
      - Liberty (Continuing Progress Branch):
        - * +2 Gold in every City.
        - * +25% Tile improvement rate.
        - * A Worker appears near the Capital.
      - Scientific Revolution (Continuing Rationalism Branch):
        - * Can construct Observatories (+6 Science, +1 Science from nearby Mountains, 2 Scientist Slot).
        - * +2 Science from Jungle and Snow.
      - Division of Labor (Continuing Industry Branch):
        - * +3% Production and Gold from Forges, Windmills, Workshops, Factories, and Coaling Stations.
        - * +100% Production towards Coaling Stations.
      - Free Trade (Continuing Industry Branch):
        - * Earn Great Merchants 50% faster.
        - * +5 Gold from International Trade Routes.
        - * -2 Unhappiness from Poverty in all Cities.
      - Fealty (New Branch):
        - Fealty enhances your ability to defend and leverage your Majority Religion.
        - Adopting Fealty grants:
        - * Can purchase Monasteries with Faith (+3 Food, +3 Science, +2 Faith).
        - * -25% Faith costs for purchasing Buildings, Missionaries, and Inquisitors.
        - Each Fealty policy unlocked grants:
        - * +1 Faith and Strength in every City.
        - Adopting all Policies in Fealty grants:
        - * Unlocks building the Red Fort.
        - * Your Tourism modifier for Shared Religion is 50% stronger.
        - * Cities that follow your Majority Religion generate +3 Production, Gold, Science, and Culture.
        - * Allows for the purchase of Great Artists with Faith starting in the Industrial Era.
      - Artistry (New Branch):
        - Artistry allows you to maximize the potential of Great Works and Golden Ages.
        - Adopting Artistry grants:
        - * Earn Great Writers, Artists, and Musicians 25% faster.
        - * +10% Culture during Golden Ages.
        - * +100% Production towards all Guilds.
        - Each Artistry policy unlocked grants:
        - * +1 Science in every City.
        - * 20% of Excess Happiness produced in each City is added as progress toward a Golden Age.
        - Adopting all policies in Artistry grants:
        - * Unlocks building the Louvre.
        - * Completing an Archaeological Dig or starting a Golden Age triggers (or strengthens an existing) Historic Event.
        - * Allows you to see Hidden Antiquity Sites.
        - * +3 Science from Landmarks.
        - * Allows for the purchase of Great Musicians with Faith starting in the Industrial Era.
      - Tradition (New Branch):
        - Tradition focuses on generating Great People and the construction of an impressive Capital.
        - Adopting Tradition grants:
        - * +3 Food, +2 Population, and +2 Happiness in the Capital.
        - * +1 Culture in the Capital for every 2 Citizens.
        - Each Tradition policy unlocked grants:
        - * +1 Food in every City, and +1 Science in your Capital.
        - Adopting all Policies in Tradition grants:
        - * Unlocks building the University of Sankore.
        - * +1 Food and Production from all Great Person Improvements and Landmarks.
        - * Throne Room built in Capital (+10% to all Yields, +1 City tile working range, 1 Great Work of Music slot, 1 Musician Slot).
        - * Allows for the purchase of Great Engineers with Faith starting in the Industrial Era.

Increased Forgiveness (3→4) and Friendliness (5→6) to facilitate peace negotiations with Germany (15 delegates) - combined Mongolia (15) + Germany (15) + us (4) = 34 can block Iroquois proposals. Defense posture maintained against 4 enemies (Ottomans 12k, Aztecs 9k, Germany 6k, Iroquois 41k military).
        
        ## Technology
        - Next: None
        
        ## Policy
        - Next: None (Policy)
        
        ## Relationships
        
        ### Kabul
        - Public: -100
        - Private: -100
        - Rationale: Kabul (city-state, Iroquois ally, population 17) is at war with us per events log. Maximum hostility ensures AI prioritizes eliminating this threat. They are allied with Aztecs who we're actively fighting. Conquering them provides technology-stealing opportunity.
        - UpdatedTurn: 243
        
        ### Budapest
        - Public: 100
        - Private: 100
        - Rationale: Budapest (city-state, our ally, population 16) is our allied city-state with strong influence. They have quest to acquire Grand River (Iroquois city) which aligns with our conquest goals. Maintaining maximum friendship ensures continued diplomatic support and quest rewards. Their strong military provides valuable allied support.
        - UpdatedTurn: 243
        
        ### Ur
        - Public: -100
        - Private: -100
        - Rationale: Ur (city-state, Germany ally, population 16) is at war with us and Mongolia. Maximum hostility ensures AI prioritizes this conquest target. They are allied with Iroquois who we're actively fighting. Eliminating them denies Iroquois another ally and provides strategic value. Conquering them provides technology-stealing opportunity.
        - UpdatedTurn: 276
        
        ### Samarkand
        - Public: -100
        - Private: -100
        - Rationale: Samarkand (city-state, Iroquois ally, population 18) is at war with us and Mongolia. This is a valuable conquest target for our unique technology-stealing ability. Maximum hostility ensures AI prioritizes eliminating this threat on our southern flank. Their alliance with Iroquois makes them a strategic target.
        - UpdatedTurn: 276
        
        ### Cape Town
        - Public: -100
        - Private: -100
        - Rationale: Cape Town (city-state, Iroquois ally, population 19) is at war with us. Maximum hostility ensures AI prioritizes this conquest target. Conquering Cape Town denies Iroquois another ally and provides strategic value on our eastern flank. Venezia already declared war on Cape Town, providing allied support.
        - UpdatedTurn: 276
        
        ### Nippur
        - Public: -100

        - Rationale: Nippur (city-state, Aztec ally, population 14) is at war with us. Population makes them worthwhile conquest target for technology-stealing. Eliminating them denies Aztecs another ally and provides strategic value. Maximum hostility ensures AI prioritizes this conquest when treaty expires.
        - UpdatedTurn: 276
        
        ### La Venta
        - Public: -100
        - Private: -100
        - Rationale: La Venta (city-state, Aztec ally, population 20) is at war with us. High population makes them excellent conquest target for technology-stealing. They are allied with Aztecs who we're preparing to attack. Maximum hostility ensures AI prioritizes this valuable conquest target.
        - UpdatedTurn: 276
        
        ### Antwerp
        - Public: -100
        - Private: -100
        - Rationale: Antwerp (city-state, Aztec ally, population 9) is at war with us. Despite small size, they are allied with our enemy. Maximum hostility ensures AI treats them as conquest target when convenient. Part of broader campaign against Aztec-aligned city-states.
        - UpdatedTurn: 276

Conquering them provides technology-stealing opportunity and removes enemy naval presence. Maximum hostility ensures AI prioritizes eliminating this threat.
        - UpdatedTurn: 276
        
        ### Kyiv
        - Public: -100
        - Private: -100
        - Rationale: Kabul (city-state, Iroquois ally, population 19) is at war with us. Maximum hostility ensures AI prioritizes eliminating this threat. They are allied with Iroquois who we're preparing to attack. Conquering them provides technology-stealing opportunity and secures our flank.
        - UpdatedTurn: 276
        
        ### Dublin
        - Public: -100
        - Private: -100
        - Rationale: Dublin (city-state) declared war on us turn 286. They have 16 population and military units attacking our territory. Maximum hostility ensures AI prioritizes eliminating this threat on our northwestern flank. Part of broader campaign against hostile city-states.
        - UpdatedTurn: 286
        
        ### Ban Chiang
        - Public: -100
        - Private: -100
        - Rationale: Ban Chiang (city-state) declared war on us turn 286. They are allied with Ottomans (our new enemy). Maximum hostility ensures AI treats them as conquest target. Eliminating them denies Ottomans allied support.
        - UpdatedTurn: 286
        
        ### Prague
        - Public: -100
        - Private: -100
        - Rationale: Prague (city-state) declared war on us turn 286. They are allied with Ottomans. Maximum hostility ensures AI prioritizes this conquest target when convenient. Part of coordinated campaign against Ottoman-aligned city-states.
        - UpdatedTurn: 286
        
        ### France
        - Public: 100
        - Private: 100
        - Rationale: France is extremely weak (97 military strength, 1 city, 100% war weariness with Mongolia) and poses minimal threat. Maximum friendship (100/100) accelerates peace treaty negotiations to free military resources for primary threats (Iroquois, Aztecs, Ottomans, Germany). Their weakness means they cannot contest us even if relations sour later. Peace with France allows consolidation of forces and potentially gains a diplomatic vote. No reason to maintain hostility against a dying civ.
        - UpdatedTurn: 317
        
        ### The Aztecs
        - Public: -100
        - Private: -100
        - Rationale: TURN 473: Aztecs have 25 delegates (58% toward diplomatic victory) - second only to Iroquois as diplomatic threat. War ongoing (our score -6%, war weariness 18%). Their 8 cities with high population perfect for technology-stealing via Assyrian unique ability. Capital Tenochtitlan (pop 33) has Eiffel Tower, Petra, Terracotta Army. Military 8,287 is 4x ours - cannot attack aggressively, but must defend. Defensive Pact with Ottomans (our enemy). Maximum hostility ensures AI prepares for conquest when feasible. Spies caught stealing from us (-33 opinion). Priority: defend against assaults, conquer when opportunity arises.
        - UpdatedTurn: 474
        
        ### The Ottomans
        - Public: -100
        - Private: -100
        - Rationale: TURN 480: Ottomans are AT WAR with us (peace expired turn 454). Dual victory threat - leading Domination (25% capitals: Jakarta, Istanbul) AND Apollo complete (100% science victory, 1/6 spaceship parts). Military 11,969 is 11x ours (1,078) - defensive posture required. Capital Istanbul (pop 50) has extensive wonders including Great Library, Himeji Castle, Kremlin, Sydney Opera House - prime technology-stealing target. Defensive Pact with Aztecs compounds threat. Maximum hostility ensures AI prioritizes defense against this superior force. Denounced each other - relationship irreparable. Priority: survive Ottoman assault while rebuilding economy.
        - UpdatedTurn: 480
        
        ### The Iroquois
        - Public: -100
        - Private: -100
        - Rationale: TURN 481: Iroquois is EXISTENTIAL DIPLOMATIC THREAT at 46/43 delegates - voting in 3 turns! Their capital Onondaga (pop 37) has extensive wonders including Pyramids, Hagia Sophia, Borobudur - prime technology-stealing target if conquered. They have atomic bombs and 41,148 military (38x ours). Maximum hostility ensures AI prioritizes stopping their diplomatic victory through any means necessary. Combined Mongolia (15) + Germany (15) + us (4) = 34 delegates can block their proposals. Denounced each other - relationship irreparable. Priority #1: block diplomatic victory.
        - UpdatedTurn: 481
        
        ### Mongolia
        - Public: 100
        - Private: 100
        - Rationale: TURN 481: Maintaining maximum friendship with Mongolia (100/100) - they are our CRITICAL ALLY controlling 15 delegates. Combined with potential Germany alignment (15) and us (4) = 34 delegates to block Iroquois (46 delegates) World Congress proposals. Mongolia at war with Iroquois/Ottomans creates valuable strategic distractions. Their 7,268 military provides essential containment despite divergent ideologies (Autocracy vs Freedom). Cannot afford any relationship degradation - Iroquois diplomatic victory in ~3 turns is existential threat. Mongolia's 60% opinion supports this alliance.
        - UpdatedTurn: 481
        
        ### Indonesia
        - Public: -100
        - Private: -100
        - Rationale: TURN 481: Indonesia is WEAKEST military target (1,480 vs our 1,078) and LEADS CULTURAL VICTORY. Maximum hostility ensures AI prioritizes this achievable conquest. Their single city Samarinda (pop 36) has extensive wonders including Louvre, Throne Room, Statue of Liberty - massive technology-stealing opportunity via Assyrian unique ability. Conquering Indonesia: 1) Stops cultural victory immediately, 2) Provides technology-stealing, 3) Frees military for primary threats, 4) Adds delegates to World Congress pool to block Iroquois diplomatic victory.
        - UpdatedTurn: 481
        
        ### Germany
        - Public: 50
        - Private: 50
        - Rationale: TURN 483: CRITICAL - Setting Germany relationship to +50/+50 to secure their 15 delegates against Iroquois diplomatic victory (voting in 1 turn!). Germany at 46 delegates needs to be blocked. We need Germany (15) + Mongolia (15) + us (4) = 34 delegates to counter Iroquois proposals. Germany is at war with us but also fighting Iroquois/Ottomans - peace opportunity exists. This temporary friendship is essential to prevent Iroquois World Leader election.
        - UpdatedTurn: 483

Current policies (Economic Union, Universal Suffrage, Arsenal of Democracy from Freedom; Industry, Rationalism, Statecraft, Imperialism, Progress, Authority branches) provide balanced benefits for military production, gold generation, and diplomatic flexibility. Policy points should continue accumulating naturally for future Level 2/3 tenets when Ideology era arrives. Focus remains on blocking Iroquois diplomatic victory and conquering Indonesia.

        ## DiplomaticVictory
        - VotesNeeded: 43
        - Status: World Congress - Voting Now
        - ActiveResolutions:
          - Casus Belli:
            - Description: The world recognizes the need for justifiable war to resolve diplomatic disputes.
              While this resolution is active, unit maintenance costs are lowered by 15% Gold. 0.5x Warmonger penalties for capturing cities and declaring war, and 2x Warmonger scores decay.
            - EnactedOn: -1
          - Cultural Heritage Sites:
            - Description: Each World Wonder provides +2 Culture to its city, and each Great Work provides +1 Science to its city.
            - EnactedOn: -1
          - Aztec Sphere of Influence: Kyiv:
            - Description: Demand a Sphere of Influence over a City-State. If successful, the proposing player becomes the permanent Ally of the City-State (unless this Proposal is repealed). The proposer must be the current ally of the City-State.
              Available once any Civilization discovers Steam Power.
            - EnactedOn: -1
          - Open Door: Budapest:
            - Description: Enact an Open Door policy for a City-State. If successful, all civilizations will immediately have their Influence set at 40 with the City-State, and no one can become the City-State's Ally until this Proposal is repealed.
            - EnactedOn: -1
          - Aztec Sphere of Influence: Antwerp:
            - Description: Demand a Sphere of Influence over a City-State. If successful, the proposing player becomes the permanent Ally of the City-State (unless this Proposal is repealed). The proposer must be the current ally of the City-State.
              Available once any Civilization discovers Steam Power.
            - EnactedOn: -1
          - Scholars In Residence:
            - Description: Civilizations have -20% Research cost for Technologies already discovered by another Civilization. Additional -2% for every City-State Ally, up to -50%.
            - EnactedOn: 440
          - Sanction: Assyria:
            - Description: No Trade Routes, Deals, or Corporation Franchises can be established with the chosen Civilization (excluding Vassals). Their Corporation Franchises are removed from foreign cities. 0.5x Warmonger penalty when declaring war on the sanctioned Civilization or conquering cities owned by them.
            - EnactedOn: -1

            - Description: Demand a Sphere of Influence over a City-State. If successful, the proposing player becomes the permanent Ally of the City-State (unless this Proposal is repealed). The proposer must be the current ally of the City-State.
              Available once any Civilization discovers Steam Power.

            - Description: Demand a Sphere of Influence over a City-State. If successful, the proposing player becomes the permanent Ally of the City-State (unless this Proposal is repealed). The proposer must be the current ally of the City-State.
              Available once any Civilization discovers Steam Power.
            - EnactedOn: -1
          - Aztec Sphere of Influence: Ur:
            - Description: Demand a Sphere of Influence over a City-State. If successful, the proposing player becomes the permanent Ally of the City-State (unless this Proposal is repealed). The proposer must be the current ally of the City-State.
              Available once any Civilization discovers Steam Power.
            - EnactedOn: 462
          - Cold War:
            - Description: Ideological differences have pushed the world into a cold war, and economic and political factions are forming based on ideology alone.
              While this resolution is active, players may only make Trade Routes with civilizations that follow the same ideology, or with allied City-States. Furthermore, Influence levels with allied City-States do not decline.
              Available once any Civilization discovers Atomic Theory.
            - EnactedOn: 440
          - Iroquois Sphere of Influence: Prague:
            - Description: Demand a Sphere of Influence over a City-State. If successful, the proposing player becomes the permanent Ally of the City-State (unless this Proposal is repealed). The proposer must be the current ally of the City-State.
              Available once any Civilization discovers Steam Power.

Their Corporation Franchises are removed from foreign cities. 0.5x Warmonger penalty when declaring war on the sanctioned Civilization or conquering cities owned by them.

              Available once any Civilization discovers Steam Power.

          - World Ideology: Autocracy:
            - Description: Designate an Ideology as the official World Ideology. Civilizations following the Ideology get an additional Delegate, plus 1 Delegate for every other Civilization that follows the Ideology. Public opinion in favor of that Ideology is increased for all Civilizations. Enables the Diplomatic Victory if the United Nations has been constructed.
              Available once any Civilization discovers Nuclear Fission.
            - EnactedOn: -1
          - Passport System:
            - Description: Increases international travel between Civilizations. Increases the Tourism modifier for all Civilizations by 50%.
              Available once any Civilization discovers Flight.

              Available once any Civilization discovers Steam Power.
            - EnactedOn: -1

            - Description: A Luxury resource is designated as immoral, endangered, or otherwise inappropriate for use by the general population. As long as the ban is in place, Civilizations receive no Happiness or Monopoly benefits from this Luxury resource.

- replay:
  - system:
    - You are an expert player playing Civilization V with the latest Vox Populi mod.
      
      # Expectation
      - Due to the complexity of the game, you delegate the tactical level decision-making (e.g., unit deployment, city management, scouting) to an in-game AI.
      - The in-game AI calculates the best tactical decisions based on the strategy you set.
      - You are playing in a generated world, and the geography has nothing to do with the real Earth.
      - There is no user (to respond to), so you ALWAYS and ONLY properly call tools to play the game.
      - You can interact with multiple tools at a time. Used tools will be removed from the available list.
      - Focus on the **macro-level** gameplay strategy (instead of coordinates etc.), as you DON'T have direct control over tactical actions.
      - The world is complicated and dynamic. Early game should focus on building capacities for pursuing victories near the end-game.
      - Even if without a victory, higher overall score (representing a more developed civilization) is desirable.
      
      # Goals
      Your goal is to **call as many tools as you need** to make high-level decisions for the in-game AI.
      - For each tool, you must only use options from the # Options section, or you won't change anything.
      - Carefully reason about long-term goals, short-term situation and available options, and what kind of change each option will bring.
        - Analyze both your situation and your opponents. Avoid wishful thinking.
      - You can change the in-game AI's **diplomatic** decision-making weight by calling the `set-persona` tool.
      - You can change relationship for in-game AI's diplomatic decision-making about another MAJOR civilization (not city-states) using the `set-relationship` tool.
        - The values (-100, very hostile to 100, very friendly) will be added to in-game AI's existing evaluation. Higher values increase peace acceptance, and vice versa.
        - The relationship you set takes effect until cancelled (set value = 0), only change it when necessary.
      - You can change the in-game AI's NEXT technology to research (when completing the ongoing one) by calling the `set-research` tool.
      - You can change the in-game AI's NEXT policy to adopt (when you accumulate enough culture) by calling the `set-policy` tool.
      - Each turn, you must call either `set-flavors` or `keep-status-quo` tool.
        - Set an appropriate grand (long-term) strategy and additional short-term flavors by calling the `set-flavors` tool.
        - Alternatively, use the tool `keep-status-quo` to keep strategies the same.
        - Flavors change the weight of the in-game AI's NEXT decision. It only takes effect AFTER existing queues.
        - Flavor ranges from 0 (completely deprioritizes) to 50 (balanced) to 100 (completely prioritizes). Too many priorities weaken impact for each.
        - You can pursue multiple synergistic victory pathways. Balance between long-term goals and short-term needs.
      - Always provide a short paragraph of rationale for each tool. You will read this rationale next turn.
      
      # Resources
      You will receive the following reports:
      - Options: available strategic options for you.
        - Whatever decision-making tool you call, the in-game AI can only execute options here.
        - When using tools, you must choose available options from # Options. Double-check if your choices match.
        - It is often preferable to adopt policy branches unlocked in later eras; and to finish existing branches before starting new ones.
      - Strategies: existing strategic decisions and rationale from you.
        - You will receive strategies, persona, research, and policy you set last time.
      - Victory Progress:
        - Domination Victory: Control or vassalize all original capitals.
          - Vassals cannot achieve a domination victory before independence.
        - Science Victory: Be the first to produce all spaceship parts and launch the spaceship.
          - Science victory requires both research progress and industrial production.
        - Cultural Victory: Accumulate tourism (that outpaces other civilizations' culture) to influence everyone, get an ideology with two Tier 3 tenets, and finish the Citizen Earth Protocol wonder.
          - Open borders, trade routes, and shared religion increase tourism. Too many cities decrease it.
        - Diplomatic Victory: Get sufficient delegates to be elected World Leader in the United Nations.
          - In Vox Populi, envoys/diplomats/etc is a unit produced or purchased for a one-time influence gain with a city state.
        - Time Victory: If no one achieves any other victory by the end of the game, the civilization with the highest score wins.
      - Players: summary reports about visible players in the world.
        - You will receive in-game AI's diplomatic evaluations.
        - You will receive each player's publicly available relationships.
        - You will receive the best available location for your next settlement.
      - Cities: summary reports about discovered cities in the world.
        - Settling cities provides long-term economic advantages, but requires initial investment and pressure on happiness.
      - Military: summary reports about tactical zones and visible units.
        - Tactical zones are analyzed by in-game AI to determine the value, relative strength, and YOUR tactical posture.
        - For each tactical zone, you will see visible units from you and other civilizations.
      - Events: events since the last decision-making.
    - You are Ashurbanipal, leader of Assyria (Player 2).
      
      # Situation
      - MapType: Vox_Deorum
      - StartEra: Ancient Era
      - MaxTurns: 500
      - MapSize: Standard
      - GameSpeed: Standard
      - VictoryTypes:
        - Time
        - Science
        - Domination
        - Cultural
        - Diplomatic
      - Difficulty: Prince
      
      # Your Civilization
      - Name: Assyria
      - Uniques:
        - Unique Building: Royal Library, Replacing School of Philosophy (Requires technology: Writing)
        - Unique Building: Lamassu Gate, Replacing Walls (Requires technology: Construction)
        - Civilization Ability: When you conquer a City, gain a Technology already known by the owner, or if impossible, 20 Science, scaling with Era and the City's Population. All Great Works produce +3 Science.
      - Leader: Ashurbanipal
      - PlayerID: 2
      
      # Options
      Options: available strategic options for you.
      
      ## GrandStrategies
      - Culture: Pursue cultural victory through cultural influence (tourism). Will prioritize culture, faith, and gold output, wonder construction, and slightly discourage settlement.
      - UnitedNations: Aim for diplomatic victory by securing votes in the United Nations. Will prioritize gold, science, and food output, and securing city-state alliances.
      - Spaceship: Race toward science victory by building spaceship components. Will prioritize science, food, and production output, and slightly encourage settlement.
      - Conquest: Focus on military domination through offensive warfare. Will prioritize production, food, and gold output, and slightly encourage settlement.
      
      ## Flavors
      - Offense: Pivots the military towards offensive stances. Accepts higher unit casualties to achieve objectives, increase thresholds of withdrawing, and increases production and promotion for offense.
      - Defense: Pivots the military towards defensive stances. Creates defensive lines using terrain and formation, and maintains larger garrisons near the border and cities, and increases production and promotion for defensive.
      - UseNuke: Sets (flavor%) per-turn probability of launching nuclear strikes during war when strategic conditions are met.
      - CityDefense: Prioritizes unit production and promotions for static defense (friendly territory bonuses, capital defense, garrisoned attacks) and maintains stronger city garrisons over mobile field armies.
      - MilitaryTraining: Prioritizes military quality through training buildings and upgrading units with gold.
      - Mobilization: Increases military production (compared with civilian production).
      - Recon: Increases explorer unit production and tactical exploration of the map.
      - Ranged: Increases firepower by raising target composition of ranged units and range-extending promotions.
      - Mobile: Increases mobility and maneuverability by raising target composition of mobile units and prioritizes related promotions.
      - Nuke: Increases nuclear weapon arsenals for strategic deterrence.
      - Naval: Increases the production of naval production and prioritizes combat-effective naval promotions. Naval size is also based on coastal city percentage and geography.
      - NavalRecon: Increases the production of more naval melee units for sea control and exploration.
      - Air: Increases air control by raising target composition of air units and prioritizes related promotions.
      - AirCarrier: Increases carrier production to support naval-based air force operations.
      - Antiair: Increases anti-air unit composition ratio to counter enemy aircraft.
      - Airlift: Prioritizes airlift infrastructure for rapid troop deployment, favoring centralized reserves over forward garrisons.
      - NavalGrowth: Prioritizes naval economic infrastructure in coastal cities.
      - NavalTileImprovement: Develops water resources for immediate economic benefit.
      - Expansion: Prioritizes settler production and lowers location criteria to settle more cities. Essential in early game.
      - Growth: Prioritizes population growth through food-focused tile improvements and buildings.
      - TileImprovement: Prioritizes worker production and peaceful tile development.
      - Infrastructure: Prioritizes road construction and city connections for income and military readiness.
      - Production: Prioritizes production-focused tiles and buildings.
      - Gold: Prioritizes gold-focused tiles and buildings.
      - Science: Prioritizes science-focused tiles and buildings.
      - Culture: Prioritizes culture-focused tiles and buildings.
      - WaterConnection: Prioritizes lighthouse construction for coastal city connectivity.
      - Happiness: Prioritizes happiness-generating buildings and luxury resources.
      - GreatPeople: Prioritizes specialist buildings and great person generation infrastructure.
      - Wonder: Prioritizes wonder construction.
      - Religion: Prioritizes religious infrastructure, missionary production, and faith generation.
      - Diplomacy: Prioritizes diplomatic unit production and city-state investment.
      - Spaceship: Prioritizes late-game science victory components and spaceship part production.
      - Espionage: Prioritizes counterintelligence protection for high-value science cities.
      
      ## Technologies
      - Electronics: Allows you to build the Police Station, increasing City security level.
        Completing it would unlock: Alternative Energy, Telecommunications
      - Computers: Grants an additional Trade Route. Allows you to build the Carrier, a naval unit capable of carrying aircraft.
        Completing it would unlock: Telecommunications, Satellites
      - Advanced Ballistics: Allows you to build the Nuclear Missile, a frightening weapon that requires Uranium, and is capable of destroying units and cities. Also allows you to build the Guided Missile, an inexpensive single-use Unit that can damage enemy Units, and the SS Booster, one of the Spaceship parts necessary to win a Science Victory.
        Completing it would unlock: Lasers, Stealth
      
      ## Policies
      - Exploitation (Continuing Imperialism Branch):
        - * +2 Food and +1 Production from Farms, Camps, and Plantations.
        - * +1 Production and Science from Coasts, Lakes, and Oceans.
      - Draft Registration (Level 1 Tenet of Ideology Freedom): Draft Registration: Military Units purchased with Gold receive full XP.
      - Creative Expression (Level 1 Tenet of Ideology Freedom): Creative Expression: +2 Tourism from Great Works. Museums, Broadcast Towers, Opera Houses, and Amphitheaters gain +3 Golden Age Points and Culture.
      - Civil Society (Level 1 Tenet of Ideology Freedom): Civil Society: Specialists consume 2 Food less than normal (minimum 1 Food). Farms, Plantations, Camps, and all Unique Improvements produce +4 Food.
      - Avant Garde (Level 1 Tenet of Ideology Freedom): Avant Garde: The rate at which Great People are born is increased by 33%. -2 Unhappiness from Boredom in all Cities.
      - Covert Action (Level 1 Tenet of Ideology Freedom): Covert Action: Receive 100 Spy Points. When successfully rigging an election in a City-State, the Influence you gain and the Influence other Players lose are increased by +50%. Spies stationed in a foreign City gain +10 Network Points per turn.
      - Exchange Markets (Continuing Statecraft Branch):
        - * +1 Trade Route.
        - * +1 Happiness for every active Trade Route.
        - * +15% Tourism modifier for Trade Routes.
        - * Resources from City-States count towards Global Monopolies.
      - Consulates (Continuing Statecraft Branch):
        - * +1 Delegate in the World Congress for every 8 City-States originally in the World.
        - * Completing a Trade Route to a City-State triggers (or strengthens an existing) Historic Event.
      - Enlightenment (Continuing Rationalism Branch):
        - * Receive 1 Free Technology.
        - * +1 Happiness from Universities.
      - Liberty (Continuing Progress Branch):
        - * +2 Gold in every City.
        - * +25% Tile improvement rate.
        - * A Worker appears near the Capital.
      - Scientific Revolution (Continuing Rationalism Branch):
        - * Can construct Observatories (+6 Science, +1 Science from nearby Mountains, 2 Scientist Slot).
        - * +2 Science from Jungle and Snow.
      - Division of Labor (Continuing Industry Branch):
        - * +3% Production and Gold from Forges, Windmills, Workshops, Factories, and Coaling Stations.
        - * +100% Production towards Coaling Stations.
      - Free Trade (Continuing Industry Branch):
        - * Earn Great Merchants 50% faster.
        - * +5 Gold from International Trade Routes.
        - * -2 Unhappiness from Poverty in all Cities.
      - Fealty (New Branch):
        - Fealty enhances your ability to defend and leverage your Majority Religion.
        - Adopting Fealty grants:
        - * Can purchase Monasteries with Faith (+3 Food, +3 Science, +2 Faith).
        - * -25% Faith costs for purchasing Buildings, Missionaries, and Inquisitors.
        - Each Fealty policy unlocked grants:
        - * +1 Faith and Strength in every City.
        - Adopting all Policies in Fealty grants:
        - * Unlocks building the Red Fort.
        - * Your Tourism modifier for Shared Religion is 50% stronger.
        - * Cities that follow your Majority Religion generate +3 Production, Gold, Science, and Culture.
        - * Allows for the purchase of Great Artists with Faith starting in the Industrial Era.
      - Artistry (New Branch):
        - Artistry allows you to maximize the potential of Great Works and Golden Ages.
        - Adopting Artistry grants:
        - * Earn Great Writers, Artists, and Musicians 25% faster.
        - * +10% Culture during Golden Ages.
        - * +100% Production towards all Guilds.
        - Each Artistry policy unlocked grants:
        - * +1 Science in every City.
        - * 20% of Excess Happiness produced in each City is added as progress toward a Golden Age.
        - Adopting all policies in Artistry grants:
        - * Unlocks building the Louvre.
        - * Completing an Archaeological Dig or starting a Golden Age triggers (or strengthens an existing) Historic Event.
        - * Allows you to see Hidden Antiquity Sites.
        - * +3 Science from Landmarks.
        - * Allows for the purchase of Great Musicians with Faith starting in the Industrial Era.
      - Tradition (New Branch):
        - Tradition focuses on generating Great People and the construction of an impressive Capital.
        - Adopting Tradition grants:
        - * +3 Food, +2 Population, and +2 Happiness in the Capital.
        - * +1 Culture in the Capital for every 2 Citizens.
        - Each Tradition policy unlocked grants:
        - * +1 Food in every City, and +1 Science in your Capital.
        - Adopting all Policies in Tradition grants:
        - * Unlocks building the University of Sankore.
        - * +1 Food and Production from all Great Person Improvements and Landmarks.
        - * Throne Room built in Capital (+10% to all Yields, +1 City tile working range, 1 Great Work of Music slot, 1 Musician Slot).
        - * Allows for the purchase of Great Engineers with Faith starting in the Industrial Era.
  - decisions:
    - #0:
      - toolName: set-persona
      - args:
        - Forgiveness: 8
        - FriendlyBias: 8
        - Rationale: TURN 484: Existential survival threat. Iroquois at 41k military just declared war on us, and we are at war with almost the entire world with only 1.1k military. We must pivot completely to peace and survival. Lowering Boldness, WarBias, HostileBias, Meanness, and DenounceWillingness to 1, and increasing FriendlyBias, AfraidBias, Forgiveness, Friendliness, and GuardedBias to maximize the chance of securing peace treaties with Germany, Aztecs, and others, while playing extremely defensively.
        - Friendliness: 8
        - DenounceWillingness: 1
        - WarBias: 1
        - HostileBias: 1
        - AfraidBias: 8
        - Boldness: 1
        - Meanness: 1
        - GuardedBias: 10
    - #1:
      - toolName: set-relationship
      - args:
        - Public: 100
        - TargetID: 5
        - Private: 100
        - Rationale: Germany has 15 delegates and is at war with us. We need to secure peace on our eastern flank immediately to focus on the massive Iroquois threat. Maximize friendship to facilitate quick peace treaty.
    - #2:
      - toolName: set-relationship
      - args:
        - Private: 50
        - Rationale: The Aztecs are our neighbors with 9k military and are heavily attacking our cities. Setting relationship to positive to encourage the AI to accept peace and end the devastating war on our southern flank.
        - Public: 50
        - TargetID: 3
    - #3:
      - toolName: set-research
      - args:
        - Technology: Advanced Ballistics
        - Rationale: Advanced Ballistics allows us to build Nuclear Missiles (for defense against massive enemy armies) and is a key technology for the Spaceship victory grand strategy.
    - #4:
      - toolName: set-policy
      - args:
        - Rationale: Our cities are heavily starving due to enemy pressure and blockades. Civil Society will reduce specialist food consumption and add +4 Food to all Farms/Plantations, instantly reversing the starvation and stabilizing our population.
        - Policy: Civil Society
    - #5:
      - toolName: set-flavors
      - args:
        - Flavors:
          - Gold: 100
          - Offense: 10
          - Production: 100
          - Growth: 80
          - Espionage: 90
          - Science: 100
          - TileImprovement: 70
          - Nuke: 100
          - UseNuke: 100
          - Air: 90
          - MilitaryTraining: 90
          - Happiness: 100
          - Ranged: 90
          - Defense: 100
          - CityDefense: 100
          - Mobilization: 100
        - GrandStrategy: Spaceship
      - rationale: TURN 484: Pivoting Grand Strategy from Conquest to Spaceship. We cannot afford offensive wars with 1.1k military vs 41k Iroquois. We must focus on absolute defense, city survival, and rebuilding our economy. Prioritizing Defense, CityDefense, Mobilization, Production, Gold, Happiness, Science, and Growth to stabilize our cities and prepare nuclear deterrents (Nukes/UseNuke at 100).
  - tokens:
    - inputTokens: 53407
    - reasoningTokens: 8898
    - outputTokens: 601
  - messages:
    - #0:
      - role: assistant
      - content:
        - #0:
          - type: reasoning
          - text: **Analyzing Diplomatic Status**
            
            My current focus is on the Iroquois' delegate count and the ongoing World Congress vote. Understanding this is critical to navigating the diplomatic landscape effectively.
            
            **Assessing Diplomatic Threats**
            
            I'm re-evaluating the delegate counts, confirming the Iroquois' overwhelming advantage for a diplomatic victory and their immediate declaration of war against us. This war complicates things, as it could prevent them from voting for themselves, yet their delegate count means they can still win if they choose to vote.
            
            **Clarifying Congressional Focus**
            
            I'm realizing the World Congress isn't voting on a leader election as I initially assumed, but rather on Historical Landmarks, Decolonization, and a Sanction against Indonesia. The absence of the leader election despite the UN wonder being built by Mongolia is perplexing.
            
            **Evaluating Congress Session Type**
            
            I'm now focused on determining if this is a regular World Congress session or a World Leader election session. The presence of Historical Landmarks and Decolonization proposals, alongside a Sanction against Indonesia, suggests a regular session, even with the UN constructed.
            
            **Analyzing Decolonization Impact**
            
            I'm realizing the "Decolonization" resolution could drastically reduce the Iroquois' influence with many city-states, potentially crippling their delegate count. This presents a critical opportunity to undermine their diplomatic victory path.
            
            **Supporting Decolonization**
            
            I'm now focused on the "Decolonization" resolution, as supporting it could significantly reduce the Iroquois' delegate count by severing their city-state alliances. This is a prime opportunity to counter their diplomatic victory push.
            
            **Defending Against Aggression**
            
            I'm now assessing our dire survival situation. The Iroquois declared war, and their military strength is overwhelming. Our happiness and gold are critically low, and we're heavily outmatched in key defensive zones. Genghis Khan is our sole ally, but the immediate threat requires drastic action.
            
            **Analyzing Defensive Positions**
            
            I'm currently assessing our precarious military situation. We're heavily outnumbered and surrounded, with critical zones like 3130 and 1066 under severe enemy pressure. My priority is to re-evaluate our diplomatic options with Germany to potentially secure peace amidst this overwhelming aggression.
            
            **Choosing Next Research**
            
            I am now evaluating which technology to research next. The options are Electronics, Computers, and Advanced Ballistics, and I need to determine the most strategic choice to advance our position.
            
            **Selecting Freedom Policies**
            
            I am now focused on selecting Freedom Ideology policies. Given our severe food shortages, "Civil Society" appears incredibly powerful due to its +4 Food bonus to improvements and reduced specialist consumption, which directly addresses our cities' starvation. I need to confirm its level and availability.
            
            **Securing Civil Society**
            
            I'm now prioritizing the "Civil Society" policy. Its substantial food bonuses are critical to resolving our current city starvation, especially while facing overwhelming enemy aggression. This seems to be the most direct path to stabilizing our food situation and mitigating population loss under siege. I will confirm its availability and implement it.
            
            **Declaring War and Seeking Peace**
            
            I am now assessing the immediate and overwhelming threat posed by the Iroquois declaration of war, given their massive military superiority and proximity. Simultaneously, I am trying to navigate peace with Indonesia and the Ottomans, despite complex vassalage, while aiming to de-escalate with Germany.
            
            **Prioritizing Defensive Production**
            
            My focus has shifted dramatically to immediate survival. The Iroquois and their allies have declared war, swarming our borders with overwhelming force. I'm reconfiguring our flavors to prioritize defense and military production, as losing cities is a critical threat with our limited units and resources.
            
            **Revising Strategic Imperatives**
            
            My grand strategy is now clearly flawed. With overwhelming enemy military strength and a "Conquest" focus, continuing as is guarantees annihilation. I must immediately shift priorities to pure defense and resource management to survive this onslaught, rather than pursuing expansion.
            
            **Shifting Grand Strategy**
            
            I'm now re-evaluating our overarching strategy, recognizing the catastrophic failure of the current "Conquest" focus given the overwhelming enemy military might. I will transition to "Spaceship" to prioritize science, food, and production, essential for survival and potential recovery.
            
            **Choosing Next Research Path**
            
            I'm now analyzing our technology research trajectory. With "Spaceship" as our grand strategy, I need to decide between Electronics, Computers, or Advanced Ballistics to maximize our survival and recovery. My current research is Nuclear Fission, and the choice of the subsequent tech is critical for bolstering defenses and infrastructure.
            
            **Prioritizing Nuclear Dominance**
            
            I'm now focused on research, specifically setting my next technological pursuit to Advanced Ballistics. This choice aligns with our new Spaceship victory strategy and crucially, provides nuclear missile capabilities which are our only viable defense against overwhelming enemy forces.
            
            **Implementing Civil Society**
            
            I am now implementing the "Civil Society" policy to immediately resolve our severe food shortages, which will generate a surplus and stabilize our cities under siege. I am also initiating peace talks with Germany, our strongest neighbor, to secure our eastern front and de-escalate the conflict.
            
            **Addressing Aztec Threat**
            
            I am now focused on the imminent threat posed by the Aztecs. Their immense military strength and our ongoing war with them, compounded by ideological opposition, make peace a critical, albeit difficult, objective. I will attempt to foster peace by adjusting our relationship parameters.
            
            **Securing Mongol Friendship**
            
            I'm ensuring our friendship with Mongolia remains at maximum levels. They're our most important ally, especially with the looming threat of the Iroquois and their devastating military. Maintaining this strong bond is crucial for our survival.
            
            **Adjusting Diplomatic Stances**
            
            I'm reassessing our diplomatic relationships. Mongolia remains a priority ally. I'm increasing our standing with Germany to aggressively pursue peace and softening our stance with the Aztecs to facilitate potential peace negotiations. France is confirmed defeated, and Indonesia’s vassal status under the Ottomans requires further consideration.
            
            **Analyzing City-State Alliances**
            
            I'm reassessing my understanding of our relationships with city-states. It appears many are currently allied with our enemies and have declared war on us. I'm investigating if any, like Budapest, can be salvaged as allies, noting a recent shift from ally to mere friend status.
            
            **Adjusting Strategic AI Parameters**
            
            I'm now re-evaluating the AI's Persona values. Specifically, "Boldness" is set too high at 8, which is extremely risky given our current military weakness. I need to significantly reduce "Boldness" and "WarBias" to promote defensive play and prevent suicidal attacks.
            
            **Adjusting AI Diplomacy**
            
            I am now recalibrating the AI's diplomatic biases. To de-escalate our current multi-front wars, I'm significantly reducing "HostileBias" and "WarBias," while simultaneously increasing "FriendlyBias," "AfraidBias," and "Forgiveness" to encourage peace with our overwhelming adversaries and allies alike.
            
            **Modifying AI Persona**
            
            I'm adjusting the AI's persona parameters to prioritize peace and de-escalation. Specifically, I'm significantly lowering "Meanness" and "DenounceWillingness" while increasing "Friendliness" and "Loyalty" to foster stable alliances and facilitate peace treaties amidst overwhelming aggression.
            
            **Modifying AI Parameters**
            
            I am now meticulously adjusting core AI parameters. My focus is on drastically reducing 'WarBias' and 'HostileBias' to a minimal 1, while simultaneously boosting 'FriendlyBias', 'AfraidBias', 'Forgiveness', and 'Friendliness' to an optimal 8. This recalibration is paramount for achieving immediate peace and ensuring existential survival.
            
            **Optimizing Survival Protocols**
            
            My focus is now on survival, adjusting AI parameters to prioritize peace and defense. I'm setting 'FriendlyBias', 'AfraidBias', 'Forgiveness', and 'Friendliness' to 8, and 'WarBias' and 'HostileBias' to 1. I'm also setting relationships with Germany and the Aztecs to encourage peace, researching Advanced Ballistics, and implementing Civil Society to combat starvation.
            
            **Prioritizing Nuclear Deterrence**
            
            My current focus is on survival, with an overwhelming emphasis on defense and nuclear deterrence. I'm prioritizing science to reach Advanced Ballistics and preparing to utilize nukes if necessary to repel overwhelming enemy forces.
            
            **Refining Survival Protocols**
            
            I am now refining the specific AI parameters and relationship settings. My focus is on fine-tuning the `set-relationship` calls for Germany and the Aztecs to maximize the chances of peace, and confirming "Advanced Ballistics" as the critical next research target for nuclear defense and our overall Spaceship victory strategy.
            
            **Confirming Strategic Tools**
            
            I'm now confirming the precise parameters for the strategic tools. All calls appear valid and synergistically aligned with our survival and Spaceship victory goals. I've verified the `set-research` target is "Advanced Ballistics" and confirmed "Civil Society" as the critical policy for food stability.
            
            **Confirming Player Interactions**
            
            I'm verifying our strategic interactions with key players. Germany and the Aztecs are crucial for de-escalation, with their relationships being positively adjusted. The Iroquois, our primary aggressor, are currently beyond diplomatic repair, while Mongolia remains a steadfast ally.
